## THAILAND FOCUS TREE
## Five political paths from the 2000 start: Thaksinist Populism (A), Royalist
## Establishment (B), Democrat Establishment (E), Reform Wave (C, a 2020-protest-era
## entry), and Greater Thailand irredentism (D, off the military branch), plus a
## Communist entry (Ghosts of the Jungle) and a shared economic/diplomatic trunk.
focus_tree = {
	id = thailand_focus

	country = {
		factor = 0
		modifier = {
			add = 25
			tag = SIA
		}
	}

	# Focus Shortcuts

	focus = {

			modify_debt_effect = yes
			country_event = thailand.50
			506 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = rubber
					amount = 10
				}
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = SIA_recover_97_util
				bonus = 1.0
				uses = 1
				category = CAT_util
			}
			# Recovery spine Step 1: float the baht. Capital flight and the export
			# collapse end (trade/min_export clear), but the IMF loan arrives with
			# interest and austerity while the revenue base craters.
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_crisis_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_trade_opinion_factor = 0.25 tooltip = trade_opinion_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_min_export = 0.08 tooltip = min_export_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_interest_rate_multiplier_modifier = -1 tooltip = interest_rate_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_consumer_goods_factor = -0.04 tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_stability_factor = 0.03 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = -0.15 tooltip = tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_economy_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_trade_opinion_factor = 0.1 tooltip = trade_opinion_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_min_export = 0.05 tooltip = min_export_tt }

		completion_reward = {

			modify_debt_effect = yes
			clr_country_flag = SIA_imf_program_active
			# Also granted by the SIA_tax_modernization decision - guard so the two
			# sources never stack the same bonus twice.
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SIA_debt_free_credibility_active } }
				set_country_flag = SIA_debt_free_credibility_active
				custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_economy_modifier }
				add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_consumer_goods_factor = -0.03 tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt }

				add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = corporate_tax_income_multiplier_modifier_tt }
				add_to_variable = { SIA_econ_population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = population_tax_income_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			}
			# Recovery spine Step 4: the early IMF payoff ends austerity, so the consumer
			# goods burden and the revenue drag lift together; the loan interest only
			# eases partway (the stability dip is the IMF crutch leaving with the program).
			custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = SIA_crisis_modifier }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_interest_rate_multiplier_modifier = -1 tooltip = interest_rate_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_consumer_goods_factor = -0.02 tooltip = consumer_goods_factor_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_tax_gain_multiplier_modifier = 0.15 tooltip = tax_gain_multiplier_modifier_tt }
			add_to_variable = { SIA_crisis_stability_factor = -0.01 tooltip = stability_factor_tt }

			add_political_power = 100
			country_event = thailand.51

	# BRANCH C — REFORM WAVE
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_seeds_of_reform
		icon = liberalization

		x = 21
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_2001_election

		cost = 5

		# Restructure: demoted out of the 2001 fork. The reform wave is a
		# later-era movement that grows out of the 2020 street protests, positioned
		# below "The Youth in the Streets" as the prerequisite of its own downstream
		# focuses (layout R2 still holds). No date gate: it opens the moment Future
		# Forward (socialism, idx 3) crosses a 5% vote share, mirroring the Communist
		# ghosts_of_the_jungle gate. Completing it hands idx 3 a decisive popularity
		# swing so the movement can WIN power; "A Future Forward" and onward only open
		# once idx 3 actually rules (SIA_reform_trunk_open).
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_reform_5pct_tt
				check_variable = { party_pop_array^3 > 0.05 }
			}
		}
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_the_2001_election }
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_seeds_of_reform"
			# This is the CAMPAIGN, not the trunk switch. Do NOT set SIA_path_reform
			# or clear the other path flags here. Doing so would strand a player still
			# committed to another trunk (their downstream locks while idx 3 does not
			# yet rule), and pre-setting the flag suppresses the realignment detector's
			# thailand.422 -> SIA_realign_to_reform when idx 3 actually wins (which is
			# what clears the old flags and strips the outgoing party's ideas). The
			# trunk switch happens on the win, not here.
			# Decisive campaign surge: hand Future Forward (idx 3) a big popularity
			# and outlook boost so it can win the next election and unlock its trunk.
			set_temp_variable = { party_index = 3 }
			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.25 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.25 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			recalculate_party = yes
			add_timed_idea = { idea = SIA_reform_spring days = 365 }
			set_temp_variable = { temp_opinion = -5 }
			change_the_military_opinion = yes
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 35
		}
	}

	####################################################################
	# BRANCH E — THE DEMOCRAT ESTABLISHMENT (idx 1, conservatism)
	# The fourth political trunk off the 2001 election. The "stays in power"

	# Democrats). Switchable like the other trunks: entry gated by
	# SIA_democrat_trunk_entry, downstream by SIA_democrat_trunk_open. A separate
	# "return to power" node (SIA_democrat_revival) hangs off the kingmaker
	# coalition further down the file.
	####################################################################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_the_democrats_endure
		icon = stability

		x = 11
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_the_2001_election

		# The Democrats (idx 1) are the start ruling party, so this entry is open
		# from 2001.1.1 unless the election unseats them.
		available = {
			date > 2001.1.1
			SIA_democrat_trunk_entry = yes
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_the_democrats_endure"
			# Clear any lingering trunk flag so exactly one SIA_path_* stays set
			# (see SIA_thai_rak_thai for the live-gate rationale).
			SIA_clear_path_flags = yes
			set_country_flag = SIA_path_democrat

	# WAVE 5 — THE FLAME REKINDLED (Communist revival path, ahistorical)
	# Position: spine at x=41 y=5 running south to y=12.
	# Space: Kra Canal occupies x=37–39; Branch D at x=47. Corridor x=40–46 is free,
	#   so the spine (x=41) carries side-forks at x=39 (left) and x=43 (right).
	# Entry gate: misery gate (SIA_misery_gate flag, ≥52 consecutive weeks stability<35%),
	#   OR 2020 crackdown + household debt path, OR republic declared + stability<40%.
	# Strategic forks (give the path real choices, not a flat spine):
	#   1. Road to power — SIA_general_strike (urban) MX SIA_protracted_peoples_war (rural).
	#   2. Consolidation — SIA_peoples_tribunals (terror) MX SIA_broad_united_front (coalition).
	#   3. Development   — SIA_peoples_army (guns) MX SIA_socialist_education (schools).
	#   4. Foreign road  — SIA_the_beijing_road (China's orbit) MX SIA_indochina_federation.
	# AI: base 0 with ahistorical escape hatch (matches Branch D pattern).
	########################

	focus = {
		id = SIA_people_seize_power
		icon = armed_revolution

		x = 0
		y = 1
		relative_position_id = SIA_general_strike

		cost = 5

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_general_strike focus = SIA_protracted_peoples_war }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL }

		available = {
			# You cannot simply click your way into power. Either the general strike has
			# forced the government to capitulate (the peaceful road, which auto-completes
			# this focus from thailand.250), or you have committed to the armed struggle.
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = SIA_seize_power_gate_tt
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = SIA_communist_peaceful_path
					has_country_flag = SIA_communist_armed_path
				}
			}
		}

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_people_seize_power"
			# Remove old-regime ideas if held (both roads)
			SIA_remove_barami = yes
			SIA_remove_three_pillars = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_network_monarchy_idea }
				remove_ideas = SIA_network_monarchy_idea
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_kings_moral_authority }
				remove_ideas = SIA_kings_moral_authority
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_peoples_constitution }
				remove_ideas = SIA_peoples_constitution
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = SIA_junta_government }
				remove_ideas = SIA_junta_government
			}
			# The armed road takes power by force: the deposed establishment does not
			# concede, it fights. Split the country BEFORE converting our own government
			# so the old regime breaks away as its own nationalist and royalist rebels
			# rather than a mirror of the new red state. ruling_party = communism keeps
			# us (the loyalists) as the communist bloc, while ideology = nationalist arms
			# the deposed royalist and military establishment against us.
			# The peaceful road (general strike capitulation) skips the war entirely, the
			# establishment there conceded rather than fight.
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = SIA_communist_armed_path }
				start_civil_war = {
					ruling_party = communism
					ideology = nationalist
					size = 0.45
				}
				add_war_support = 0.10
			}
			# Formalize the communist state on our side: exact party index, name, leader
			# and suspended elections (thailand.70-style protocol). On the armed road this
			# runs after the split, converting only the loyalist half; on the peaceful
			# road it is the whole bloodless takeover.
			set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 4 }
			set_temp_variable = { disable_elections = 1 }
			change_ruling_party_effect = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_leader_SIA = yes
			# World reactions fired from the seize-power event
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = { id = thailand.251 days = 3 }
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
		}
	}

	# ─── v0.9 Develop conquered land — ASEAN hegemony payoff ───
	# The claim focuses stake the whole region but end at wargoals. This capstone
	# turns a conqueror's map into an empire that holds: it opens the reconstruction
	# decisions (pacify -> develop -> core) over every seized province and grants the
	# occupation apparatus, so the New Order can actually be administered rather than
	# just declared. Reachable from either the maritime or the mainland claim branch.
	focus = {
		id = SIA_the_new_order
		icon = irredentism

		x = 0
		y = 3
		relative_position_id = SIA_asean_hegemony

		cost = 7

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_maritime_claims focus = SIA_mainland_claims }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_the_new_order"
			set_country_flag = SIA_reconstruction_unlocked
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SIA_occupation_administration } }
				add_ideas = SIA_occupation_administration
			}
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -20 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_productivity_change = -30 }
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { SIA_is_reclaimed_state = yes }
				add_compliance = 10
				state_flat_productivity_change_effect = yes
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
	}

	# ─── v0.9 Develop conquered land — Western Frontier payoff ───
	# The subdue campaigns end at a wargoal with no follow-up. This convergence
	# capstone turns held frontier ground into governed ground: it opens the
	# reconstruction decisions (pacify -> develop -> core) and grants the
	# occupation apparatus that drives them. It also seeds compliance and
	# infrastructure on any frontier state already held so the loop has something
	# to build on.
	focus = {
		id = SIA_frontier_integration
		icon = national_unity

		x = 2
		y = 3
		relative_position_id = SIA_burma_breakaway_question

		cost = 6

		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_subdue_the_shan }
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_subdue_the_karen }
		prerequisite = { focus = SIA_subdue_the_wa }

		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_POLITICAL FOCUS_FILTER_STABILITY }

		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus SIA_frontier_integration"
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -8 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_country_flag = SIA_reconstruction_unlocked
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SIA_occupation_administration } }
				add_ideas = SIA_occupation_administration
			}
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = { SIA_is_reclaimed_state = yes }
				add_compliance = 15
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			add_political_power = 75
			add_stability = 0.03
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
